About The Episode:
Briar Cochran runs a $2–3 million-a-year content agency with just three employees. His AI-powered systems help clients generate roughly a quarter-billion views per month and have cut content ideation from 15 hours to 15 minutes. In this episode, Briar shows you how to use AI agents to find proven ideas, create better content, save hours of work, and grow a personal brand.
